**AC1 both insurance types** (BG refused exactly like GKV); **AC3** the whole bulk refused, only the missing-IK VO listed and the has-IK control neither listed nor validated; **AC5** the insurance gate (PKV and Privat Basis at the SAME Gesellschaft and therapy type are validated with no IK and get their invoice) and the status gate (`for_fixing` / `cannot_validate` live there too); **AC4** from the footprint — entity 6's Ergotherapie IK saved, `99970-1` resolving to it, validated, in submission 75 with invoice R626-1. **AC1's therapy-type rule is read-only:** `99967-1` is a **speech-therapy VO with a physiotherapy therapist** at a Gesellschaft with **no** Logopädie IK, and it resolved to the **Physiotherapie** IK and joined that submission — so a rule reading the VO's own type would have refused it; at scale, every validated VO whose two types differ resolves by the DEPARTMENT. **Coverage gap, measured not assumed:** the MIRROR direction (a therapist whose department has no IK on a VO whose own type does) has **0 instances** of the 1,261 unvalidated GKV Fertig-Behandelt VOs, so only the allowed direction is exercised. **END GOAL over the whole book: exactly ONE validated GKV/BG VO has no IK** — `965112-2`, Curano Hannover / Physiotherapie, validated **2026-08-13** — and it is asserted PRE-fix, partitioned at the deploy (#3651/#3709) rather than as a flat zero, since the Out of Scope keeps already-validated VOs. **The #3821 cross-check separates the two causes exactly:** of the **8** validated GKV/BG VOs in no submission, 1 is missing-IK and **7 hold the right IK** — the second, unexplained cause this suite reported on #3821 — and that count has grown 7 → 8 since (`100258-1`), so it is still producing stuck VOs. **THE PM'S ONE FAIL HAS SINCE BEEN FIXED, and only the bundle shows it:** their 3 Oct run marked AC-1 row 3 / AC-2 bullet 1 FAIL for a regular Admin, because the edit form stripped the payload to an admin whitelist keyed on the **NEW** validation status, so the status never reached the server and the save reported success; **`0eae51982` (2026-10-04, the day after) keys it on the STORED one** and is deployed — the minified guard reads `'validated'===z?.validationStatus`, a **distinct variable with an optional chain** where the callback's own argument is `t` (`values` is never optional, so the `?.` is the tell). The server refuses a regular Admin identically (**422 on both paths**), which is what makes it a one-line client change. **MORE TRAPS:** `entity`, `entity.id` and `billingBatchCount` are **silently ignored** as `/prescriptions` filters (each returns the whole 35,964-row book), so the population is narrowed on `validationStatus` + `insuranceType`; **`/errors/missing-ik` occurs 0 times in the bundle** — the client keys off `missingIk` — so a probe written on the type URI reads as not-deployed; the German is escaped (`ist keine IK-Nummer f\xfcr`, plain **0** / escaped 1, #3611's trap in the direction that fakes a pass) and a control literal proves the helper can find one; `GET /billing_batches/{id}` answers **422** (#3288's readiness check) and a batch IRI renders as `/billing_batches/{id}/optica-export`, so a submission number comes from the COLLECTION; and **`test.setTimeout()` does not reach a `beforeAll`** (#3375) — the 2,020-row walk died at the 90 s default and all 11 tests reported "did not run". **The ticket's own Steps to Reproduce are STALE:** they name Curano Berlin-Brandenburg 3 and VO 2706-27 / 2707-27, but entity 3 now holds `ikSpeechtherapy` and 2707-27 is in submission `S-2026-457-001` — both the doing of **#3821's Part 2 run** on 30 Sep. Also worth knowing the commit fixes two things no AC mentions (the Android ToastHost claiming the toast queue under v1 screens, and every failed VO save showing two error toasts). Manual notes in `docs/manual-test-3822-missing-ik-validation.md` — chiefly a re-test of the regular-Admin edit forms on screen, which nobody has done since the follow-up fix. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.missing-ik-validation.page.ts` |
| `sa_discharge_billing_check.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @DischargeBillingCheck @ReadOnly @Mutating` | RC 3.15 (#3808, commit `a0b961b4cbb` / PR #3861) the billing validation gains **"Entlassmanagement-Timing"**, failing a GKV discharge VO whose carried-out sessions lie outside the **7/12-day window** and listing those dates. The follow-up to **#3830's AC9**, which deliberately left the billing check on the ordinary 28/14-day deadline until this shipped. **Deployed; all five ACs verified, 9 passed, 0 `fixme`.** **Mostly read-only** — the registry, the fixtures, their sessions and their stored verdicts all answer without a write, because a stored verdict IS the engine's own answer; **one test writes, and undoes itself by construction**. **THE DEPLOYMENT PROBE IS THE REGISTRY:** `GET /validations` serves the new row — **id 55 `billing_discharge_management_timing`, timing `billing`, severity `error`, category `time`, insurance `['public']`** — and a `timing: billing` discharge row could not exist before. **A NEW DESCRIPTION SHIPPED rather than a second row under the creation key**, which is the safer of the two options the Developer Reference set out: older migrations update by description alone (`WHERE description = 'discharge_management_timing'`) and would otherwise hit both rows. **The billing row covers `blanko` and the creation row (id 14) does not** — deliberate, since the rule applies to every VO marked Entlassmanagement as the RC 3.14 expiry rule does; both kinds lists are asserted so the divergence cannot drift. **THE DEVELOPER REFERENCE'S OPEN QUESTION IS ANSWERED:** it asked someone to check how the Validierungskonfiguration screen lists **two rows with the same label**, since both keys resolve to "Entlassmanagement-Timing" — it shows both, under **different section headings** (`Erstellungsprüfungen` / `Abrechnungsprüfungen`), so they are distinguishable and nothing needs doing. **AC4'S NOTE IS API-GENERATED, so it is NOT in the frontend bundle** — searching there for `Behandlungen außerhalb des Zeitraums` returns **0**, which reads exactly like it was never implemented; it is served as **`autoNote`** on the verdict row and is **omitted when the check passes**, so a key list taken from a passing row does not show it either. The note is **rebuilt independently and compared verbatim**, so a check that flags the right VOs but names the wrong dates still fails (#3576's technique): `Entlassmanagement: Behandlungen außerhalb des Zeitraums (Ausstellung: 01.09.2026, Beginn bis: 08.09.2026, Ende: 13.09.2026): 14.09.2026.` **AC3's six rows all land exactly** on the PM's purpose-built block (all issued 01.09.2026): 100017-1 (2,5,8) passes, 100018-1 (2,13) passes because **13 Sep IS day 12**, 100019-1 (2,14) fails on 14.09, 100021-1 (3,15,20) fails on both, 100022-1 (8,10) passes because **8 Sep IS day 7**, and 100023-1 (9,11) fails on **BOTH** — the half a naive "after day 12" rule gets wrong, since a late start invalidates the whole VO. **AC2's session-counting rule is tested discriminatingly**, because 100024-1 / 100025-1 / 100026-1 carry the **same** out-of-window date (20 Sep) and differ only in kind: planned → passes, refused **without** signature → passes, refused **with** signature → **fails**; plus 100027-1 (planned only) passing as "no carried-out session". AC1's applicability table over all five insurance cases, and 100037-1 proving a **discharge date changes nothing** (identical verdict AND note to 100019-1), and 100032-1 proving the entry ORDER does not leak into the note (entered 20, 02, 14 → listed 14.09, 20.09). **AC5 IS THE ONE WRITING TEST AND IT IS SELF-RESTORING BY CONSTRUCTION:** `PATCH /prescription_validations/{id}` `{passed:true}` is the manual **Pass** and `POST /prescriptions/{id}/check-billing-validation` is **Erneut prüfen** — which puts the automatic `false` and its note straight back, so **the undo IS AC5 row 2**; row 3 then removes the Entlassmanagement marker (`PATCH /prescriptions/{id}`), re-checks to a pass, and restores the marker and the verdict in a `finally`, with the end state asserted equal to the start. Observed and pinned: the verdict row is **updated in place** (same id throughout), not recreated — which differs from what #3830 saw on an anonymized VO. **TRAPS:** the verdict filter is the bare **`prescription=`** while **`prescription.id=` is accepted and SILENTLY IGNORED** (18 rows against 200), so every VO appears to carry a hundred verdicts belonging to others; and **"no stored verdict" does NOT mean "the check does not apply"** — 100020-1 / 100033-1 / 100034-1 / 100036-1 carry **zero** verdicts for *any* check because they are Aktiv, issued 4 Oct and never validated, so an oracle reading absence as "not applicable" reports four false mismatches (mine did, first pass), and a fixture whose marker is the point of it (100028-1 is the GKV **not-marked** case) must be read rather than assumed. **FINDING** → reported as a green test: **the Localization Reference's worked example for VO 9489-1 is stale.** It lists six session dates (17.07, 20.07, 24.07, 27.07, 31.07, 03.08); the VO now holds **two** carried-out sessions and the note correctly reads `…: 17.07.2026, 20.07.2026.` — so a QA comparing the note against the ticket literally sees a mismatch that is not one. The example describes the 23 Sep production-copy snapshot. Manual notes in `docs/manual-test-3808-discharge-billing-check.md`. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.discharge-billing-check.page.ts` |
| `sa_expiry_hint_running_vos.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @ExpiryHint @TBoardV2 @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 (#3849, commit `76f76ff451d` / PR #3864) the T Board v2 hint **"N VOs laufen in 14 Tagen aus"** must count only VOs that are still running, and must leave a Folge-VO out while the VO it follows is still running. **Deployed on both halves; 8 passed, 0 `fixme`. Read-only** — every API request a GET; the screen is navigated and read, and the one control operated is the hint's own "Diese anzeigen", a client-side filter chip that writes nothing. On the production copy of 25 Sep, **88 closed VOs sat in the hint for 45 therapists**. **BOTH HALVES HAVE A PROBE.** The API half adds `Prescription::getFollowUpParentStatus()` — a NEW transient field in the `therapist-board:read` group, set by `PrescriptionGroupingService` beside `isFollowUp` — which could not exist before the fix, so its presence IS the deployment probe: measured **423 follow-up rows, 423 carrying a parent status, 0 non-follow-up rows carrying one**. That field is also the whole reason AC2 is implementable at all, since the parent is usually NOT on the board (**43 of the follow-up rows have no predecessor on the board**) — a parent that has just closed is exactly when the follow-up starts counting, so the status had to be served rather than inferred from the other rows on screen. The app half is `HINT_PREDICATES.expiring` in `useBoardRows.ts`, which drives BOTH the count and the rows "Diese anzeigen" lists — one predicate, so **AC3 holds by construction** and is verified as such. **The oracle is the board's OWN payload** (`GET /therapist-prescription-groups?therapist=`, the #3471 technique), with the shipped predicate ported: on Sara Fischer's board **58 rows sit in the 14-day window and 37 are counted, dropping 21 — every one a closed status** — and the painted hint reads `37 VOs laufen in 14 Tagen aus`, lists **37** rows, and the computed set matches it **exactly, 0 differences in either direction**. The pre-fix count (58) is asserted to be strictly larger, so the number is shown to have MOVED rather than merely to look plausible. **TWO POOL TRAPS, and each one alone makes the fix read as doing nothing or as over-counting.** (1) **Each patient group carries TWO arrays** — `prescriptions` and **`completed`** — and the closed VOs, the entire subject of this ticket, are in `completed`; reading only `prescriptions` yields 41 of 41 counted and "dropped 0", i.e. the fix appears inert (that is exactly how the first measurement here went). (2) **Four statuses are never listed by the board at all** — `Bereit`, `For Review`, `Pending`, `Sent Back to Therapist` — so they must leave the pool BEFORE the predicate runs; leaving them in gives **41 against the painted 37**, and the four surplus rows are the PM's own fixtures 100166-1 / 100167-1 / 100173-1 / 100174-1, each of which is **in the window and still running** so that only "the board does not list it" keeps it out. That is not part of the fix — it is unchanged board behaviour since 9 Aug 2026, and **the PM corrected AC1's wording on 5 Oct 2026 to say precisely this**, which is the day this was tested. **The fixtures are the PM's own block** (Sara Fischer, 100160-1 … 100175-1, all validity 12.10.2026) and every one is **re-read before use**, because two had already moved since their run of 4 Oct — 100168-1 and 100169-1 both read `Fertig Behandelt` now. **The predicate is additionally driven as a pure function at every case**, including the deliberate **FAIL-OPEN**: a follow-up whose parent status is null or unreadable is COUNTED, because an over-count is a row the therapist can dismiss while an under-count is work that never reaches them — a port that read "unknown" as "running" would under-count and read as the fix being too aggressive — plus an unrecognised VO status counting as running, and the window's bounds inclusive at both ends (0 and 14 in, 15 and −1 out). **COVERAGE GAP, measured rather than assumed, and it is AC2's own exclusion half:** across six boards there are **44 follow-up VOs with a still-running predecessor and NOT ONE has a validity date inside 14 days**, so the rule has a population but the exclusion is never exercised on live data. **The PM's fixture for it was consumed** — their notes record 100169-1 as `Bereit` (running), which made 100169-2 the held case, and it now reads `Fertig Behandelt`. Restoring it is one field: set **100169-1** back to a running status and the hint should fall 37 → 36 with 100169-2 leaving the list, since it is Aktiv and 7 days out. **AC4 (tablet) is not driven** — this suite has no tablet target; the PM covered it on 2 Oct and the predicate is shared app code, so the risk is in the rendering rather than the rule. **TRAP reaching the board at all:** the saved `storageState` is single-use (#3460) and **`UntreatedDaysPage` does not mint**, so the board lands on the login form and the "Therapeut:in wählen" picker never appears — which reads as the picker having been removed; `mintUiSession` first. Manual steps and the fixture table in `docs/manual-test-3849-expiry-hint.md`. Uses `Pages/therapist/therapist.expiry-hint.page.ts` |
| `sa_patient_form_warnings.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @PatientFormWarnings @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 **#3806** (a warning when a GKV insurance number looks wrong, PR #3833) and **#3807** (the duplicate warning also matches on last name + birth date, PR #3831) — tested in one file because they share `PatientForm.tsx`, surface in the same three places, and #3810's own notes point out all three messages can appear on that form together. **Both deployed; every AC verified that can be, 9 passed, 0 `fixme`. READ-ONLY — no patient is created.** **WHY TWO TICKETS ABOUT CREATING PATIENTS NEED NO PATIENT: both checks are READS the form issues while someone types**, so each AC table is driven against the endpoint the screen itself calls (#3576's technique) — `GET /patients/insurance-number-check?number=` answering `{matchType: bsnr\|lanr\|null}`, and `GET /patients/duplicate-check?firstName=&lastName=&lenient=1&birthDate=` answering members that each carry a `matchType`. **#3806's OTHER HALF IS CLIENT-SIDE AND IS PORTED FROM THE DEPLOYED BUNDLE RATHER THAN FROM THE AC**, because the rule survives minification verbatim: `y=/^[A-Za-z]\d{9}$/`, `N=(e,n)=>'public'===n&&/^\d{9}$/.test(e.trim())`, `f=(e,n,t)=>{const s=e.trim();return'public'!==n\|\|''===s?null:null!==t?t:y.test(s)?null:'format'}`. That is AC1 and AC2 entire in four lines — the trim, the empty case, the insurance-type gate, the format regex, and **`null!==t?t`, which IS AC1's "the BSNR or LANR warning replaces the format warning"**; the spec asserts the regex and the precedence FROM the bundle, so the truth table runs against the shipped source. `N` also explains why a correct number costs no request: the server is asked only for values that are **exactly nine digits**, so `A123456789` never triggers a lookup. **AC1's precedence is exercised on a genuine collision** — `723253500` is nine digits, so it fails the format test AND is a BSNR, which is the case the rule exists for (without the lookup it reads `format`, with it `bsnr`). **THE FIXTURES ARE SELF-VALIDATED BY WALKING, AND THAT IS NOT OPTIONAL: `/practice_bsnrs?number=` and `/doctors?doctorId=` are BOTH silently ignored**, returning the whole collection (1,459 and 1,946) exactly like a bogus key — so "I filtered and found it" proves nothing. Walking also settles what a filter could not: each fixture is **unambiguous** — `723253500` is a BSNR and nobody's LANR, `820665702` a LANR and no practice's BSNR — and a number that were both would leave the expected warning undefined by the AC. `773748000` is an **ADDITIONAL** BSNR (`isMain: false`), the half of AC1's "main or additional" clause a main-only lookup would miss. **TRAP: never use a plain 9-digit number as a "format only" example** — `123456789` is itself a BSNR on staging and answers `bsnr` (the PM hit this too). **#3807 is verified as its own AC3 table, all eight rows** through the form's check: Mueller/Muller/trailing-space/swapped/hyphen → `name_dob`, "Anna Maria"/"Peter" → the new `last_name_dob`, and a different birth date → nothing — with both Match values required to occur, or the table would be satisfied by one of them and AC1's two-row mapping never exercised. **AC2's seven normalisation rules** (ä/ae/a, ö/oe/o, ü/ue/u, ß/ss, case, spaces anywhere, hyphens) each driven against the patient it must find, all `name_dob`. **AC1 in one reading** via the PM's G pair: one existing patient sharing the first name and one not, labelled `name_dob` and `last_name_dob` in a single response. **The regression direction the Testing Guidance itself worries about** is pinned — the fold was added AFTER today's mapping, so Müller/Mueller, the swapped pair and the hyphen pair must still be **full-name** matches rather than demoted to last-name ones; plus an unrelated birth date still matching nothing, which stops "every pair is found" being true by accident. **A FAILED ASSERTION THAT TURNED OUT TO BE THE TEST'S FAULT, AND THE CONTRACT IS MORE INTERESTING THAN THE ONE I ASSUMED:** the pre-#3807 provider returned nothing while EITHER name was under two characters; #3807 must relax half of that, since its whole point is matching regardless of first name — and it relaxed exactly that half. **Last name under 2 characters → nothing, as before; a short or EMPTY first name → the last-name matches still come back and no full-name match.** Keeping the old guard on the first name would have silently disabled the new rule for anyone who types the last name first, so both directions are now asserted. **Also fixed here:** the page object retries a THROWN transport error as well as a 5xx — a `socket hang up` throws before any status exists, so a retry written only around `status >= 500` never fires and one blip cascades the whole serial describe (#3872 hit the same thing). **Two more silent-filter instances recorded:** `/patients?birthDate=` is ignored too. #3806 AC4 (save with the warning showing) and #3807 AC5 ("Kein Duplikat bestätigen" then create) both require a real `POST /patients` and are in `docs/manual-test-3806-3807-patient-form-warnings.md`, along with the note that **AC3 rows 4 and 5 now return TWO members each** because AC5's own test created those patients — correct, but a QA following the reproduction sees two rows where the ticket's table implies one. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.patient-form-warnings.page.ts` |
| `sa_ready_billing_private.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @ReadyBillingPrivate @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 (#3796, PR #3823) Privat Basis VOs leave the Admin-Performance **Bereit zur Abrechnung** tile once invoiced, like PKV VOs, and an expired PKV or Privat Basis VO with carried-out sessions can get a first invoice. **Deployed; all six ACs verified, 8 passed, 0 `fixme`.** #3709 made it urgent by expiring Privat Basis VOs automatically from RC 3.14, so an expired treated private VO would otherwise have sat on the tile with no way to invoice it. **NOTHING IS WRITTEN, and on this ticket that is a constraint rather than a convenience:** almost every AC is about CREATING AN INVOICE, an invoice consumes a number that is never released, and cancelling one leaves a `cancelled` row for good (#3449). The file creates none — and does not need to, because the PM's 3 Oct run left ~20 fixtures and a dozen invoices on staging and **each carries a dated, attributable footprint**, which turns most of the ticket from "needs a write" into "read the record" (the #3821 technique). **AC1 IS A PURE DATA PREDICATE AND IS CHECKED IN BOTH DIRECTIONS AT FULL SCALE:** **158** PKV/Privat Basis VOs hold a live invoice and **0** are on the tile, while the **44** private rows that ARE listed hold **0** live invoices — and the discriminating subset is the **10 Privat Basis VOs with a live invoice, all absent**, which is the deployment probe, because the old exception was `insurance_type != 'private'` so every one of them would have been listed before. **ASK THE INVOICE BOOK, NOT THE VO BOOK:** the converse is one walk of **622 invoices**, each row embedding its VO with `insuranceType`, where a per-VO walk of the ~1,300 PKV VOs did not finish in two minutes. **THE PROVENANCE FLAG SEPARATES ALL THREE ROWS OF AC2**, which is what makes them readable without a write — `invoice_logs` stamps `meta.type` on `invoice_created` (#3426), so whether a human asked for an invoice is a fact: **99816-1** expired in the nightly run at **20:02:51**, was validated at **00:26:56**, and `R126-139` was created **AUTOMATIC at that same second** (AC2 row 1 — the behaviour #3655 deliberately blocked, so its existence IS the change), against **99815-1**, validated *before* its expiry, which got **nothing at the expiry** and then `R126-136` **MANUAL** four hours later (rows 2 and 3). **AC5 IS PROVEN BY TWO INVOICES THE PRE-FIX CHECK COULD NOT HAVE PRODUCED**, which is stronger than watching a check pass: the old guard priced the VO's PRESCRIBED Heilmittel at the PKV tariff, so **99978-1** (Privat Basis on **BGM**, which has *no PKV price*) computed 0,00 € and was refused on every route — it now holds `R126-141` at **51,96 €** (2 × 25,98 €) — and **99979-1** (Privat Basis **Blanko**, **0 prescribed units**) computed 0,00 € under *either* tariff and now holds `R126-142` at **59,26 €**. **AC6 is read out of one save's `batchId`:** `d840709b` carries `insuranceType privat_basis->private` AND `validationStatus ->validated` in the same second, followed at once by an automatic `validated->undefined` reset, and the VO holds **exactly one** invoice, created by the LATER save — with `R126-143` the **immediate successor of R126-142**, so the type-change save burned no number. AC3/AC5 row 3 over four zero-session VOs spanning **three statuses** (Abgelaufen, Archiviert, Fertig Behandelt), all holding 0 invoices; AC1 row 5 with three GKV VOs holding live invoices still listed plus the BG fixture, which is the clause a widened exception breaks; AC4 an expired validated GKV VO with sessions holding no copayment invoice. **THE TRAP THAT MAKES AN ABSENCE MEANINGLESS:** a VO can be off the tile for reasons unrelated to this ticket — #3775's population is the three end statuses, ≥1 signed session and **in no billing batch** — so the two cancelled-only VOs off the tile (**5714-3, 3277-4**) are excluded for being batched, and the GKV control **99977-1 left the tile when validating put it in batch 63**, which is exactly what the PM recorded under AC-4 having seen it listed beforehand; the spec asserts that each off-tile VO is excluded by one of those reasons rather than treating absence as evidence. **More traps:** a Storno is not a live invoice (exclude `cancelled` AND the reversal, #3449); the signed-session predicate reads **`rejectedTreatment` / `rejectedTreatmentWithSignature`** and the short names do not exist on an Activity, so a predicate on them excludes nothing (#3649, #3814); and on `/invoices` the per-VO key is the **bare `prescription=`** while `prescription.id=` is silently ignored — the exact opposite of `/prescription_billing_batches` (#3821). **For the PM, outside this ticket:** the development team found that **Privat Basis invoices are never sent to DATEV and their payments never picked up** (both daily jobs select GKV and PKV only); it is split to **#3818**, shipping with this one, and is worth confirming on the same build before the production run since this ticket increases the number of such invoices. The writes the suite declines — AC2 row 1 on a fresh VO, the nightly-expiry rehearsal (`app:prescription:expire`, **non-Blanko only**, since a Blanko VO past its 16 weeks goes to *Fertig Behandelt* and is invoiced in the same run), and AC6 in one save (Super Admin only — a regular Admin cannot validate on the VO edit form) — are in `docs/manual-test-3796-ready-billing-private.md`. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.ready-billing-private.page.ts` |
| `sa_practice_write_roles.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @PracticeWriteRoles @Security @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 (#3869, fix `ff7f527bf`) creating or changing a practice becomes **Admin-only**, matching the rule that already governed deleting one and editing its BSNRs. **Deployed; all four ACs verified plus the Out-of-Scope neighbours, 7 passed, 0 `fixme`.** Before it, `Post` and `Patch` on `Practice` carried no operation-level `security` and fell through to the resource's `ROLE_USER`, so a therapist token was accepted. **The exposure is concrete rather than theoretical:** `Practice::setPracticeId()` renumbers the main `PracticeBsnr` row and removes other rows holding that number, so a therapist PATCH of `practiceId` reached exactly the rows the BSNR endpoints protect. **VERIFIED AS A MATRIX OF STATUS CODES AND NEVER BY WRITING** — the standing rule for an authorization ticket (#3550, #3549, the tech-DD P1 batch). Every probe is one of three safe shapes: refused, which by definition writes nothing *and a refusal that ever stops being one IS the vulnerability*; aimed at an id matching nothing, so there is nothing to write even when authorization passes; or an empty merge-patch whose no-change is asserted by a full before/after body diff. **No practice is created, renamed, renumbered or deleted.** **THE ABSENT-ID PROBE IS THE KEY, and it works because authorization runs BEFORE the object is fetched:** `PATCH /practices/99999999` answers **403 for a therapist and 404 for an admin**, so ONE request separates the roles and writes nothing either way — without it, showing "the admin is still allowed" would mean mutating a real practice. **THE MESSAGE, NOT THE STATUS, SAYS WHICH RULE FIRED**, which is what a bare-403 check cannot do: the fix ships its own wording (`Only administrators can create practices.` / `…change practices.`) beside the pre-existing `…delete practices.`, while `/practice_bsnrs` and `/practice_contacts` keep Symfony's generic `Access Denied.` — so a therapist's 403 is attributable to the NEW gate rather than to something that was always there. Verified: **AC1** the full table — therapist refused on create/change/delete with each shipped sentence, **both admin roles** reaching the handler (create **422** at validation, change and delete **404** at the lookup), BSNR writes still refused generically, and reads still 200 for all three roles, which is the clause a blunt resource-level lock would have broken; **AC2** the ticket's own Steps to Reproduce driven field by field — `notes`, `practiceId` (the BSNR exposure) and `name`, each sent at its **CURRENT value** so even a build whose gate had failed would write nothing — all 403, with practice `723253500` **byte-identical before and after** and the refusal naming no caller; **AC3** both admin roles' empty merge-patch accepted **200** with the body unchanged and the count still 1,464; **AC4** the therapist's dropdown search, list, detail and count all 200 over a populated list. **The three roles are asserted from their own tokens before anything else** (`ROLE_THERAPIST` / `ROLE_ADMIN` without super / `ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN`), because AC1 separates Admin from Super Admin and a run using one account for both would leave half the table unexercised — and because `ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN` inheriting `ROLE_ADMIN` is a claim about `security.yaml` that only two distinct tokens can test. **Out of Scope verified with ONE probe shape across three sibling resources**, which is what makes the answers comparable: `/practices` refuses with the new wording, `/practice_contacts` refuses generically (already admin-only, so demonstrably the old gate), and `/practice_activities` answers **404 for the therapist AND the admin** — no role gate, so the change did not leak onto it. **TRAP: an empty body is not a universal probe.** `POST /practice_activities` with `{}` answers **500 for every role**, discriminating nothing and reading like a server fault rather than an authorization answer; the absent-id form answers a clean 404 there instead. Prove the probe shape works on a resource before reading a role rule out of it. **For the PM:** AC4's example names "the Practice field in the VO form", a screen **a therapist cannot open on the web** (the route is admin-only and unchanged here) — the PM raised the same point, and the reads behind any picker are checked instead. Also worth knowing that **there is no audit trail for this at all** — `practice` has no `created_by`/`updated_by` and there is no practice change log — so "only admin screens ever wrote practices" cannot be measured after the fact and rests on the ticket's code read, which is exactly why a regression here would leave no trace in the data and the guard is worth having. AC3's field-changing save (practice form create/edit, CRM notes) is a UI claim the PM verified by saving; it is recorded in `docs/manual-test-3869-practice-write-roles.md` rather than driven here, because saving means changing a real practice. **Run at `--workers=1`** — three tokens per run, and #3462 throttles `POST /auth` at 5/min per username. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.practice-write-roles.page.ts` |
| `sa_discharge_vo_marker.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @DischargeMarker @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 (**#3819** the Entlassmanagement badge + **#3820** Folge-VO "Bestellen" at creation), shipped as `04df30f59aa` (app, `Ref #3819`) and `b103c748dbc` (api, `Ref #3820`) on `release/3.15.0`, 2026-09-26 — **neither names its issue in the subject**, so a commit search on the number finds nothing and both are pinned by sha. **#3819 deployed and verified on both Admin Board surfaces; #3820's positive path has NO staging fixture and its deployment is not client-decidable. 8 passed, 0 `fixme`.** **Read-only** — every request a GET; the board is navigated, searched and read. **THE HEADLINE IS A GUARD NO AC STATES.** `b103c748dbc`'s own message records it: *"a discharge VO at a facility that orders its own follow-ups (praxis_vo / er_bestellt_selbst, #2617) keeps its status blank, as the nightly ordering run does"* — and **every discharge VO created on staging since the deploy is at `QA Test ER`, a `praxis_vo` facility**, so all five sit behind it with a blank status, which is correct behaviour and looks exactly like the ticket not working. A PM following the ticket's own QA steps on 99699-1/99700-1/99701-1/99703-1/99709-1 sees no "Bestellen" and would report it as broken. Staging holds **14 self-ordering facilities of 268** — the one the QA fixtures live at is one of them. The guard's two readings are asserted to AGREE on every discharge VO (the facility's `orderingMode` vs the VO's `orderingStatus`), which is the claim the commit makes. **#3820's deployment is therefore NOT decidable from a client, and the file says so rather than guessing:** the commit adds no route and no serialized field, `/status` gives the release not the commit (#3704), and with the whole population guarded "deployed" and "not deployed" are indistinguishable — the same wall as #3719. **TWO ASSERTIONS OF MY OWN THAT WERE WRONG AND ARE WORTH THE SPACE.** (a) An AC6 check of *"the order date predates the deploy, so nothing re-stamped it"* **fails on a correct build**: #3800's nightly expiry sets a blank Folge-VO status to Bestellen as it expires a VO, so 99664-1 (created 09-25) carries an order date of **09-26** and 99671-1 one of **10-01**, both after this fix shipped and neither written by it. AC6's real evidence is the VOs that were already blank and are blank still (2 of 14) — a backfill would have moved them. (b) A loose `Entlassmanagement` substring over the logs matches **#3800's expiry reasons** (*"Entlassmanagement: Behandlungsbeginn nicht innerhalb von 7 Tagen…"*, 10 of them) and reports this fix as having fired; the exact reason **`Entlassmanagement-VO: Folge-VO sofort bestellen`** occurs **0** times. And AC5's "never re-evaluated" must count moves across **both** log types — the expiry writes `field_change` on `followupStatus`, not `follow_up_status_change`, so reading only the latter returns 0 on every VO and passes without testing anything (measured: 8 VOs, 8 moves, exactly one each). **#3819 verified on screen:** the badge paints on the Admin Board row, and AC1's *"next to the VO number"* is asserted as the **CELL** — it is STACKED UNDER the number inside the VO-number cell (`"9594-1Entlassmanagement"`, 180×38px, number at y=295 and badge at y=315), exactly as the Verstorben badge sits under the patient name, so a left-to-right adjacency check finds no VO number on the badge's own line and reports AC1 failing on a correct build. The 180px is the commit's own widening (v2 rail 112 → 180). **AC3 is measured against the badge it names**: same pill height, same radius (999px) and same font size as Verstorben, with a **different** background — `rgb(229, 236, 245)` informational against the error pair — and red asserted not to dominate. **AC2's control needs a practice that issues BOTH kinds** (the AC calls this out: 18 of 30 hospital practices on the production copy have both) — practice **1610, the ticket's own, has exactly ONE VO** and cannot serve, so the control is **755 JhenQA Medical Center** (31 discharge / 154 normal), and the control row is asserted to be PAINTED before "no badge" is read as evidence, since the board hides Archiviert and a statusless VO never renders. **TRAPS:** **do not probe the bundle for `DischargeManagementBadge`** — minification renames a component, so it reads 0, and `DeceasedBadge` (shipped for releases) reads 0 too, which is the control that makes the zero meaningless; **`isDischargeManagement` is not a registered filter** (`=true`, `=false` and a bogus key all return the whole 35k book), so the population is walked and the flag read per row — **46 discharge VOs across 15 practices** since 2026-04-01; the flag is `isDischargeManagement` on `/prescriptions` but **`dischargeManagement`** on `/v2/prescriptions`; the collection is **id-ascending**, so every discharge VO sits at the END and a first-page slice reports 0 of them (#3704); and **`/v2/prescriptions?patient=` is SILENTLY IGNORED** (byte-identical to unfiltered and to a bogus key) while **`patient.id=` works** — reading "that patient's VOs" the wrong way returned four VOs of four unrelated people and the deceased fixture picked from them carried no Verstorben badge at all. `practice=` and `practice.id=` both work, so neither collection's convention predicts the other's (#3550). #3820's AC1/AC2/AC3 and #3819's AC2 tick/untick → `docs/manual-test-3819-3820-discharge-marker.md`: ticking is **one-way per VO** (AC4 says unticking leaves the status alone), so it would leave a real VO in "Bestellen" on the list the billing team works from. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.discharge-vo-marker.page.ts` |
| `sa_entity_ik_instant_save.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @EntityIkSave @ReadOnly @Mutating` | RC 3.15 (#3815, PR #3840 + the AC6 follow-up PR #3945 merged 2026-10-03 23:39Z) on an EXISTING Gesellschaft an IK row's check mark and its confirmed delete each save at once. **Deployed on both PRs; all seven ACs verified, 9 passed, 0 `fixme`.** **Mutating, confined to ONE QA Gesellschaft — entity 14 "FT3815 AC7 Gesellschaft"** (the PM's own, no VOs): only its Logopädie row and, for AC6, its IBAN are written, both restored through the API in `afterAll`. The real Curano Gesellschaften are never written, because their IKs decide which VOs can be billed (#3822, #3821). **The form has NO route of its own** (`/entities` throughout) and its IK table carries testids keyed by field — `ik-add-` / `ik-edit-` / `ik-delete-` / `ik-input-` / `ik-confirm-` / `ik-saving-<field>`; the list's pencil is a `tabindex` div with no role, found by row geometry. Every save is read off the page's OWN request: **AC1/AC2** one `PATCH /entities/14` whose body is exactly `{ikSpeechtherapy: "…"}`, the message `IK-Nummer gespeichert.`, the row repainted, the API holding it; **AC6 bullet 2** "Cancel" sends nothing and the reopened form still shows the number; **AC3** the confirmation's text matched verbatim with type, number and Gesellschaft filled in and nothing sent while it is open; **AC4** Abbrechen sends nothing, Entfernen sends `{ikSpeechtherapy: null}`. **AC5 is simulated with `page.route`, so no failure reaches the server:** a 422 violation shows `IK-Nummer konnte nicht gespeichert werden: QA simulated refusal` with the row still in edit mode on the typed number; a **body-less 502** shows the `ik-saving-` spinner while the client retries (~32 s) and then **`…: HTTP 502`** — #3945's fix for the PM's "Unknown error" observation; the same check mark then saves once the route is lifted; and a refused delete leaves the IK saved. **AC6 bullet 1 — the PM's 3 Oct FAIL — is FIXED:** a typed-but-unsaved IBAN survives the IK save (the PM saw it snap back within 400 ms because the form re-seeded from the refreshed query), the IK PATCH carries the IK alone, and "Save" then sends the TYPED IBAN with the already-saved IK; the IBAN is restored straight after. **AC6 bullet 3** `99999A995` → `Only numbers are allowed`, 21 digits → `IK number must be at most 20 characters`, neither sent (still English, as before this ticket). **AC7 read-only:** on "Neue Entität" the check mark and delete only change the row — no message, no confirmation, no request — and the form is CANCELLED so nothing is created. **Trap:** a failure message stays painted, so a second failure assertion must exclude the first message's text or it reads the stale one (this file's first run "saw" the 502 after 0 s). **One page, one session** for the whole file (single-use refresh token #3460, `/auth` throttled 5/min #3462), so "reopen" is Cancel + pencil, never a reload. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.entity-ik-save.page.ts` |
| `sa_save_shows_saved_values.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @SaveRefresh @ReadOnly @Mutating` | RC 3.15 (#3797, e568e9cf2 + PR #3946 merged 2026-10-03 23:39Z) every open screen shows the saved values right after a save. **Both deployed; the PM's AC1 row 4 FAIL is fixed, and this file's own FINDING (below) is fixed too by PR #3990 — RE-TESTED 2026-10-07: 6 passed, 0 findings.** **Mutating, on the PM's own fixtures only** — facility 275 "FT3797 Dritte Einrichtung", insurer 1089, ICD code 46283 "Z99.97" — each restored through the API in `afterAll` (a stale run's `QA3797*`/`QA-*` value is repaired in `beforeAll`). Creates/deletes (AC1 rows 5/6), AC3 and the tablet (AC4) are the PM's passing rows and are not re-driven, since a create cannot be undone on these resources. **The RELOAD is the subject**, so the tab is signed in with #3761's reload-safe `seedSession()`; `mintUiSession` would land a reload on the login form. **AC1 row 4 (the PM's FAIL, a reload 0.6–1.5 s after a save showed the old value for ~70 s because the persisted cache `therapios\REACT_QUERY_OFFLINE_CACHE` was written on a 3 s throttle) is fixed:** the persisted copy now holds the new value **72–116 ms** after the PATCH answers, and reloads at **~350 ms and ~1,260 ms** both show the saved facility ID in the searched list and the reopened form. AC1 rows 1–3 on the facility and AC2 on the ICD code (at once + a reload at 500 ms). #3946's side fixes: the new empty-list wording ships, and a **10-character facility ID is refused 422** (it used to be a 500). **RESOLVED FINDING (2026-10-06 → fixed 2026-10-07 by PR #3990) — the insurer LIST stayed stale for up to 60 s after a save, from the browser's HTTP cache.** The app DOES refetch the lists the moment the PATCH answers, but `/insurance_providers` still sends **`Cache-Control: max-age=60, private`**, so Chrome answers those refetches **from its disk cache** (CDP `fromDiskCache: true`, 3 of 3) with the pre-save body; the new name was first painted at **+66 s**. The reopened FORM is right, because a PATCH invalidates the cached item URL — so a check on the form alone passes, which is likely how the PM's AC2 row recorded the insurer as PASS. Developer Reference step 4 named exactly this risk; the header moved to `no-cache` for `/elderly_care_homes`, `/entities`, `/users`, `/icd_codes`, `/treatments` but **not for `/insurance_providers` or `/doctors`** (both still `max-age=60`), so the doctor lists very likely share it (unproven: no QA-owned doctor fixture). **The fix (PR #3990):** the stale header came from a SECOND source — the resource's own `cacheHeaders` attribute, which the `CacheControlListener` fix did not cover — so `max_age => 0` was set on the editable resources (`/insurance_providers` AND `/doctors`); the response stays revalidatable (it still 304s), and an architecture test now fails any resource with a mutating operation that declares a browser `max_age`. The test that asserted the defect was converted into an AC test as designed: both endpoints now answer `max-age=0`, the post-save list refetches come back **200 from the server, 0 of 3 from the disk cache**, and the searched list shows the saved name at once. **Traps:** after a save the pre-save list screen stays in the DOM, **hidden**, carrying its own search box and row — every locator and DOM read must be visibility-filtered or it reads the stale hidden copy; and a list's search box goes **`readonly` after Enter**, so a second search reopens the list (a full load, which also restores the persisted cache — the honest read). Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.save-refresh.page.ts` |

Effectively read-only:** every write sent is one the fix must REFUSE, and each target is re-read afterwards. **AC1** a list of VO numbers returns both VOs, an unknown one is just unmatched, nested/keyed forms → 400 `exact[prescriptionId] must be a value or a list of values.` **AC2** a treatment line without its Heilmittel → **422 `prescribedTreatments[0].treatment: This value should not be null.`** (`[1]` for a second line) — sent with NO patient/therapist so even a broken build could not persist it (Symfony collects all violations in one pass, so the nested one is named beside the top-level ones); the VO book total is unchanged, and the PM's controls 100910-12/-13 carry status `N/A`. Note `numberOfTreatments` must be a STRING or the request dies at denormalisation (400) before validation runs. **AC3** `/preview` with `''`/`{bad`/`null`/`[]`/`{}`/`123`/no data → 400; GET/PUT/PATCH/DELETE → 405. **AC4** Document Center `status=zzzBogus`, `status[]`, `search[]` → 400 on all five types (a valid status still 200); parent-vo / parent-vo-candidates / child-vo-candidates with `notadate` / `2026-13-45` → 400 (valid date 200); termination with an unknown `treatmentStatus`/`followupStatus` → 400 and VO 100900-1 stays Aktiv. **AC5** from the PM's footprint: text 195 `freed_slot_offer` → `failed` / `subject_gone` at assign (offers have no client resource — `/waitlist_offers` 404s). **AC6** PATCHing practice line 47 to line 1's length → 422 `This practice already offers this treatment with this length.`, line unchanged. **AC7 on screen**, user request forced to 500 in the browser only: 4 attempts (1 + 3 retries), the message once, the form opened once and went back once, no `Maximum update depth`. **Traps:** the Team table's Aktion column sits past 1600 px in a horizontally scrolling table — at 1600 the pencil is off-screen and a click lands on nothing (use 2200); the edit form opens on the SAME `/team` route, so detect it by its title (`Edit User`), not the URL. **AC8** read from source: the log line carries `appointment_id`/`freed_slot_id`/`origin`/`send_at`, all on `PhiPatterns::ALLOWLIST_KEYS`. |
| `sa_blanko_history_heilmittel.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @BlankoHistory @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 (#3793, fix + QA fix `86b56b0b5`) the treatment history (**Dokumentation (Behandlungsverlauf)**, row → Weitere → "Doku öffnen") shows a Blanko session's codes joined with " + " and its minutes in brackets; regular VOs keep codes only; a Blanko double session shows the DOUBLED minutes on the first of its two rows. **Deployed; all three ACs verified on 7 fixtures, 2 passed. Read-only.** The oracle is the API, never a hardcoded string: codes from `activityTreatments[].treatment.code`, minutes from `calculatedTreatmentDuration` (already doubled for a V1 double, #3711). Verified, painted == derived on every row: the ticket's own **4823-6 `PFB-H-BV + HBH-E-BV + AB-E-BV + VBP-BV (60 min)`** (Stephanie Möller's board); Ergo Blanko 45 and 60 min (99825-1); PT Blanko `(20 min)` ×2 (99827-1); regular 99831-1 `KG` with no minutes; Ergo doubles 99834-1/99991-1 `(90 min)` for an entered Dauer 45 and PT double 99837-1 `(40 min)` — each on the FIRST row with the minute-less twin directly below, and the notes tag `Doppelbehandlung ×2 · 90 Min` / `· 40 Min` carrying the same doubled minutes (AC3) while the second row's tag has none. Reached as Super Admin via the T Board's "Therapeut:in wählen" picker + the board search. **Three reading traps:** the window is a portal OUTSIDE `#root`; its column headers are NOT leaves (a sort icon sits beside the text), so cells are read by their OWN text nodes and scoped to the ancestor of the title that also holds the headers; and it paints its title and a spinner BEFORE its table, so the reader returns `[]` until the headers exist — a throw would end the `expect.poll` on the first read. |
| `sa_vo_slip_linked_image.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @VoSlip @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 (#3868, fix `6a539ccff` VOSlip.tsx) the billing validation page's **VO Slip** shows the front image LINKED to the VO (the VO form's "Vorderseite Upload-ID"), falling back to the old search by the number typed at upload only when no image is linked. **Deployed; all five ACs verified, 4 passed. Read-only** — pages are opened and read. The oracle is the API: linked = `prescriptionImages[0].uploadId` on `GET /prescriptions/{id}` (the VO form's own pick), fallback = `/prescription_images?search[prescriptionNumber]=<VO>`; the slip is read as its `Upload ID` row + the title `Verordnungsbild - <id>`, and the file must have RENDERED. Verified: **AC1** 1344-5 → `186-274` and 6174-5 → `98-19`, both with a number search that finds nothing (the bug's case); **AC2** 2337-14 falls back to `251-46`, 100002-1 (neither) stays empty with "Bild hochladen"; **AC3** 100009-1 shows the linked `ADM-1333` over `ADM-1332` uploaded under its number; **AC5** 2752-20 `67-212` unchanged; **AC1 at scale** on four VOs the file DISCOVERS — linked images whose typed number is not their VO's (100003-1 → ADM-1334 over ADM-1330, 100004-1 → ADM-1336 over ADM-1335, 100032-1 → ADM-1337 over ADM-1338, 100010-1 → ADM-1339 with no number match), all showing the linked one; **AC4** search field, Vorderseite/Rückseite and the back side's own "Upload suchen" still there. **Three traps:** a **PDF** upload (ADM-1334 is `%PDF-1.7`) renders as an `<iframe>` on the S3 object, not an `<img>` — an image-only render check reports a correct slip as broken; on a re-open the slip can paint the PREVIOUS image for ~1 s, so it is polled to a STABLE matching reading; and each fixture is a fresh `page.goto`, so the session is #3761's reload-safe seed (`mintUiSession`'s single-use token lands the second navigation on the login form). |
| `sa_multi_tab_refresh.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @MultiTabRefresh @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 (#3910 AC1) tabs refreshing the login together never present the same single-use refresh token. **The PM FAILED AC1 on 2026-10-06** (3 tabs reloaded together: 3 of 20 rounds had two tabs present the SAME token → 401 → family revoked → logged out everywhere; their lock trace showed the next Web-Lock holder reading the spent token from localStorage in the same millisecond the previous holder wrote the new one). **Fixed 2026-10-07 and verified here: 4 passed** — `5da96139d5` keeps the web refresh token in **IndexedDB (`therapios-auth`)** and resolves `setTokens` only after the write commits; `bf85b72830` (API) rotates a token again instead of revoking the family when it was the family's LAST rotation, revoked < 30 s ago, with its successor still live (the "reload during an in-flight refresh" logout; amends #3459 AC2 by user decision, an accepted 30 s replay risk). Measured on the PM's own protocol (all tabs reloaded in one tick, each round gated on every refresh answering): **3 tabs × 40 rounds and 2 tabs × 20 rounds, every refresh a DISTINCT token, every answer 200, no tab logged out.** The grace window is pinned with BOTH limits: T0→T1 then T0 again → **200**, and T1 still works; but once the successor is EXCHANGED the old token is a real replay (401, family revoked), and after **33 s** the spent token is refused. #3460's `sa_auth_token_security.spec.ts` still passes unchanged because its replays are never the family's last rotation. **Harness traps:** fire the reloads from inside the pages (`setTimeout(location.reload)`) — awaiting Playwright's `page.reload` timed out on a slow staging load; seed the session ONCE per token (#3761) — the app moves it into IndexedDB on its first refresh; and a round gate can pass before a slow tab even SENDS its boot refresh ("refreshes so far: []"), so the per-tab signed-in wait is the real gate. **Observation, not reproduced:** in one earlier run, while staging was degraded (`/status` 4–7 s, an `/auth` ETIMEDOUT), two of three tabs sat on the boot spinner for >120 s in round 6 — neither logged out nor signed in; it did not recur in 40 further 3-tab rounds and is recorded without a cause. The other #3910 ACs, checked beside it (no spec): AC5 repeat add → **422** "This VO is already in this billing batch." with the join count unchanged; AC13 moving a KG session onto a day that holds one → **422 `ACTIVITY_DUPLICATE_CONFLICT`**; AC8 `/preview` order → a real PDF; AC2/AC9/AC10/AC12 read from source. **Trap that bit this run (write, restored):** the PM's AC7 had deleted the KG row from VO 100910-1's 28.09 session, so a 22.09 → 28.09 move was NOT a duplicate and was ACCEPTED — session 284562 was moved and restored within a minute; re-read a duplicate fixture's codes before relying on it. |
| `sa_vo_edit_keeps_gesellschaft.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @VoKeepsGesellschaft @ReadOnly` | RC 3.15 (#3964, fix `4780ad9ff` in both `PeopleFacilitiesSection`s) opening an existing VO no longer swaps its Gesellschaft to the Hauptbehandler's CURRENT one. The form opened empty, then loaded the VO and its therapist, which looked like a therapist change, so "therapist changed → set their Gesellschaft" fired and the next unrelated save stored the swap (117 VOs on production, logged as a MANUAL change by whoever saved). **2 passed. Read-only** — the bug lived in the FORM STATE that "Speichern" sends, so AC1 is observed on the field itself rather than by saving: on VOs whose Gesellschaft differs from their therapist's (93 on staging; one fixture per company pair), both forms (`/vo-management/<id>/edit` and `/v1/vo-management/<id>/edit`) are watched for 8 s after load — the window the swap used to happen in — and **5 VOs × 2 forms read only the VO's own Gesellschaft** (BB3, BB2, FT3805, Hannover, FT3816 against therapists of BB1/BB3). AC2: picking another Hauptbehandler of a THIRD Gesellschaft fills it (109920-2: Elena Freier → Curano Hamburg) and "Abbrechen" stores nothing (VO re-read through the API). The PM ran the real saves, 12 rows on both forms incl. creation- and billing-validating saves. **Trap:** a therapist with NO Gesellschaft passes a bare `eid <= 7` filter (`null <= 7` is true in JS); the field then correctly empties to its "Gesellschaft suchen..." placeholder and reads like AC2 failing. **Suite-wide trap found here (fixed in `Pages/util/api-token.ts::seedRefreshToken`):** since #3910 the app reads the refresh token from **IndexedDB first** (`therapios-auth` › `tokens` › `refresh`) and since `efabffb` the project storageState carries setup's IndexedDB token, which is spent after the first test — so a token seeded into localStorage alone LOSES and the page lands on the login form. `mintUiSession` and every `seedSession` copy now write IndexedDB too. |

`tests/Production/Therapist/sentry_environment.spec.ts` (`@Therapist @OfflineCache @SentryEnvironment`) covers #3385 AC6 from the production side: it reads the `environment` tag off the envelopes app.therapios.de sends unprompted and asserts `production`. It self-skips while production serves a pre-3.11 build (v3.10.0 today, tagging everything `staging` — the defect the ticket describes).

`tests/Production/SuperAdmin/sa_privat_basis_datev.spec.ts` (`@SuperAdmin @PrivatBasisDatev @ReadOnly`) is the PRODUCTION half of **RC 3.15 #3818** and is ARMED: the ticket's one first-night item is **Stornorechnung S326-6** (−630 €, VO 2978-21), reversing **R326-10**, which DATEV received on 2026-07-24 while the VO was still PKV. While production reports < 3.15 the test asserts the bug's state (S326-6 `not_sent`, no sync) — **2026-10-06: production 3.14.0, VO 2978-21 `privat_basis`, R326-10 `synced`, S326-6 unsynced** — and once production reports 3.15 it requires S326-6 `synced`, so re-run it after the first 21:30 UTC night on 3.15. Read-only, every request a GET. **Observation, not #3818:** R326-10 is served at **520,90 €** with float dust against its Storno's 630 € — a live recompute of a cancelled legacy-window invoice (#3604's shape); the DATEV reversal posts the Storno's own 630, which is what DATEV booked.

`tests/Production/SuperAdmin/sa_session_count_parity.spec.ts` (`@SuperAdmin @SessionCountParity @ReadOnly`, two tests also `@Slow`) covers **RC 3.13 #3704** — an old-style Doppelbehandlung must count as TWO completed sessions and a fee-only visit as ONE, on the five VO list views. **Verified on production 2026-09-16; the fix is live; 6 tests pass.** **Read-only — every request a GET except the one `POST /auth` that mints the read token**, and the on-screen test only navigates and reads. **Five views, one field:** the Admin Board list, its mobile card, the Dokumentation window, the Patient Management prescription table and the GKV Unbatch view all read **`activityCount` on `GET /v2/prescriptions`**, computed by its own SQL in `PrescriptionListProvider::batchComputed()`; `bd2da1cb6` rewrites that one expression and nothing else, so checking the field checks all five. **`/status` cannot settle deployment here** — both API halves report `3.13.0`, which is the release and not the commit — so BOTH expressions are ported and the served count is compared against each; the one it matches is the one deployed. The pre-fix `COALESCE(treatment_qty, 1-or-2)` reached its fallback only when the LEFT JOIN was NULL, i.e. when the visit had **no `activity_treatment` row at all**, so a V1 double at quantity 1 took `treatment_qty = 1` and never doubled and a fee-only visit took 0. **Censused, not sampled, on the ticket's main population:** a full walk of all **39,477** VOs finds **1,006** old-style double VOs, 893 with visits, of which **832 are affected (5,846 visits, +5,849 sessions)** — and **893 of 893 serve the post-fix count, 0 the pre-fix one, 0 mismatches**; a random **10,000-VO** sample of non-double VOs (25.3% of the book) adds **7,678 of 7,678 matching**. AC1's own example VO **8950-1** reads 10/10 with 0 open against a pre-fix 9, and on screen the Beh. Status column painted **`26 / 36`** for the Aktiv VO 8512-2 where the pre-fix rule gives 13 — read off the board's OWN captured `/v2/prescriptions` response (#3471's technique), so the painted number is compared to the payload it came from. AC3: all 9 V2 VOs with visits are unmoved. AC4: 1,300 rejected-without-signature and 81 planned visits all contribute 0, and the start/last date aggregates still derive from the same visits. **Two figures for the PM, both larger than the ticket's:** it states 663 VOs / 4,345 visits (measured: **832 / 5,846**) and **37 fee-only visits** — 65 turned up in a quarter of the book, ~250 book-wide — and the fee-only cases are worse than "one visit": VOs **3206-5** and **4838-7** have *every* visit fee-only, so pre-fix they read **0** completed sessions, 4838-7 being a finished 6-of-6 VO showing 0. **An edge no AC covers:** the shipped "no treatment-kind row" branch doubles on `double_treatment` **without** checking V2 — faithful to `Activity::getActivityCountContribution()`, but it would move a V2 VO, against AC3; **0 instances exist on production**, so it is latent and is reported rather than asserted. **Traps:** `activity.doubleTreatment` is omitted when false and arrives as `true`, `false` or absent on the SAME VO, so only `=== true` is safe (#3602); `/v2/prescriptions` returns a **bare array** with no `totalItems` (the book size comes from `/prescriptions` under `ld+json`); **`doubleTreatment`/`doubleTreatmentV2` are silently IGNORED as filters** there — filtered and unfiltered pages come back with byte-identical row ids (#3449's shape) — so the population must be walked; `/activities` registers **`prescription[]`**, which takes a batch of ids and is the only reason a census is affordable; and the collection is **id-ascending, so Aktiv VOs are at the END of the book** — a slice from page 1 is archived history, and a screen test fed from there skips for want of a live fixture (`walkRecentVos()` exists for exactly that). Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.session-count-parity.page.ts`

`tests/Production/SuperAdmin/sa_risk_rows_and_dates.spec.ts` (`@SuperAdmin @ProdRiskRows @ReadOnly`, two tests also `@Slow`) covers **RC 3.14 #3814** (the ready-for-billing dates count from the LAST SIGNED TREATMENT) and **#3803** (the risk table lists each VO ONCE with all its risks) from the PRODUCTION side. **Both verified live on 2026-09-29; 4 passed, 0 `fixme`, in 54 s.** **STRICTLY READ-ONLY, and that decides what is checkable:** every request is a GET and the board is navigated and read. **`POST /kpis/orga/risks/export` is deliberately not called** — it persists nothing, but it is a POST, so on production it is left for a human; the cost is stated rather than glossed, namely that #3814 AC5's *Fertig seit (Tage)* COLUMN and #3803's CSV can only be seen through it. Both rules are verified anyway, because that column IS `daysSince` and the CSV's one-row-per-VO is the same grouping the screen paints. **NO PATIENT DATA:** the payload carries real `patientName` values on production, and nothing in the file reads, asserts on or logs them — every assertion is a count, a date or a VO number, and the screen reader takes only the group header (therapist, count, value), never a VO row. **The production API is `https://api.app.therapios.de`** — `api.therapios.de` does not resolve, and the host is discoverable only from the deployed bundle (5 references). It reports **3.14.0**. **#3814 needs no export because the ticket reduces to one question:** `daysSince` is the Status/Frist figure and `statusDate` is the date it counts from, so the whole AC is *is `statusDate` the last signed treatment?* — answered by reading each VO's own activities. Measured over a 120-VO deterministic spread of the 396-row tile: **120 of 120 match the signed-treatment predicate while the naive "last treatment of any kind" matches only 115**, so **5 discriminating VOs** carry the evidence, and an anti-vacuity guard requires the naive predicate to do strictly worse (without it the test passes against a build that just took the last activity). AC2 over the whole tile: all three end statuses carry a value — **Abgebrochen 131/131, Fertig Behandelt 219/219, Abgelaufen 46/46** — where before #3814 the column was filled only for finished VOs; and the tile's own >30-day rule still holds on the corrected date (min `daysSince` **31**, 0 rows at ≤30). **THE TRAP THAT PRODUCED FOUR FALSE MISMATCHES before it was understood:** the signed-treatment fields are **`rejectedTreatment` / `rejectedTreatmentWithSignature`**, NOT `rejected` / `rejectedWithSignature`. The shorter names do not exist on an Activity, so a predicate built on them excludes nothing, silently degrades to "last treatment of any kind" and reports mismatches on a correct build — 4 in the first 60 VOs, every one a later REJECTED session. #3649's negative test with the field names that matter. **#3803 is verified on the screen against the payload as an oracle**, since the provider is unchanged (it still serves one row per VO PER TILE) and the change is the client-side grouping: **all 148 painted groups match the distinct-VO count, and 100 of them discriminate** — i.e. would show a different number under the pre-fix row counting (Σ distinct **5,869** against Σ rows **6,192**). The complement is asserted too: with the ready tile selected the two counting rules have **0** disagreements, because within one tile a VO appears once — which is why #3803's AC8 could freeze that download — and **323 VOs carry more than one risk**, the population that makes the no-tile view differ at all. Both anti-vacuity guards are explicit. **Traps:** `/kpis/orga/risks` wraps `tiles`+`rows` in a SINGLE Hydra member, so the ordinary `member` unwrap yields one element and a row scan finds nothing (#3774); the readiness gate waits for a painted GROUP, not for the section or the column headers, which paint first and make `paintedGroups()` return `[]` — reading exactly like the table not being grouped; and a deactivated therapist paints `"<name> (Inaktiv)"` while the payload carries the bare name, with stored names not clean (a double space collapses in HTML), so both sides go through `normalizeName()`. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.prod-risk-rows-dates.page.ts`

`tests/Production/SuperAdmin/sa_patient_merge_list.spec.ts` (`@SuperAdmin @ProdPatientMerge @ReadOnly`) covers **RC 3.14 #3804** (the patient merge command runs a reviewed list given at run time) from the PRODUCTION side. **Checked 2026-09-29: 3 passed, 2 gated. The verdict is that the fix is deployed and the one-time operations run is still outstanding** — which is the ticket's own post-deploy step, so it is reported rather than failed. **READ-ONLY and PII-minimising by construction.** Every request is a GET; nothing runs the command, which is console-only. A `patient_merged` log's `meta` carries a real person's **name, birth date and insurance number**, so the page object strips those at the boundary — `MergeLog` models only the numbers and the meta KEY NAMES, which means no assertion, log line or trace can carry them. Patient records are read for existence and VO count only. **The 'not yet run' verdict is established two independent ways, and they must agree:** (1) the audit trail — production holds **92 `patient_merged` logs and all 92 are from 2026-08-05**, #3131's one-off batch, with **0** naming AC6's pair and **0** merges since; (2) the records themselves — patient numbers **8181** and **8864** still resolve to **different** patient ids, each still holding its own VOs. A merge would collapse them to one. **THE NUMBER-VS-ID TRAP IS PINNED ON PRODUCTION DATA, because getting it wrong reads a stranger's record rather than failing.** The ticket and the list CSV both say "patient 8181 / 8864" and the CSV column is literally named `patientId` — but that is the UI patient NUMBER (`patient.patientId`), not the API id: on production **number 8181 is api id 6922** and **number 8864 is id 7630**, so `?id[]=8181&id[]=8864` cheerfully returns two unrelated real patients (it did here, mid-investigation). **`?patientId=` is silently IGNORED on `/patients`** — every value returns all **9,715** rows — so the cheap correct route is the VO number, since a VO is `<patientNumber>-<n>` and `?prescriptionId[]=` IS a registered multi-value filter; one request resolves both records without walking the collection. Same family as #3790, where the same mistake resolved to a different real patient. **The two AC tests are GATED on the run having happened, not `fixme`'d**, so the file begins verifying the moment operations executes the command with no edit: AC3/AC4 assert every post-#3131 merge log is system-written, records who was removed, and writes exactly one entry per merged record; AC6 asserts 8864 merges into 8181, the merged number no longer holds VOs of its own, and the survivor's VOs all sit on one record. Staging coverage of the same ticket (the dev team's 25 Sep run, 7 passed) is in `tests/Staging/SuperAdmin/sa_patient_merge_list.spec.ts`. **The operations list itself holds names, birth dates and insurance numbers and lives only in `s3://therapios-csv-files`** — it must never reach Drive, Slack, the ticket or this repo, and nothing in either spec touches it. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.prod-patient-merge.page.ts`

`tests/Production/SuperAdmin/sa_discharge_window.spec.ts` (`@SuperAdmin @ProdDischarge @ReadOnly`, one test also `@Slow`) covers **RC 3.14 #3830** (a discharge VO SHOWS day 7 / day 12) and **#3800** (it EXPIRES on those days) from the PRODUCTION side. **Checked 2026-09-29: 4 passed, 1 `fixme` — and the headline is a SPLIT between the two tickets.** **READ-ONLY:** every request a GET. The nightly command is never run, and #3830's AC6 creation-check preview is skipped here because it is a POST (it is transient and writes nothing, so it stays in the staging spec). **#3830 IS live on production:** of the discharge VOs issued since 2026-08-01, **all 24 in-scope ones show `treatmentStartDeadline` = issue+7 and `validityDate` = issue+12**, the **1 PKV** one carries neither (excluded from expiry entirely, #3709), and there are **0** BG / Privat Basis / no-insurance-type ones — exactly what #3830's own "Expected effect" forecast. AC2 holds on the closed population too (18 closed across Abgelaufen and Abgerechnet, all still +7/+12). **#3800 is NOT firing:** in a 1,672-entry expiry-log tail spanning 2026-07-28…09-28 there are **ZERO** `Entlassmanagement:` reasons, while **4 open discharge VOs sit past their window with no expiry** (10469-2 `window12` due 09-23, 10786-1 `start7` due 09-23, 10807-1 `window12` due 09-27, 10792-1 `window12` due 09-28) — three of them due BEFORE the last nightly run. **The control is what makes that evidence rather than a guess, and it is its own test:** the job demonstrably RUNS — **1,523 automatic expiries** in the tail, newest **2026-09-28T20:02:24** (one day old), **249** of them carrying #3651's German `Behandlungsbeginn nicht innerhalb von N Tagen` — so "no discharge expiry" cannot be explained by the job being switched off, and #3651 is deployed. Both commits are on `release/3.14.0` and the shared `App\Util\DischargeWindow` is demonstrably in the build, since the shown dates come from it — so the cause needs a developer and is not guessed at here. The AC1/AC6 test is `fixme`'d against that finding with the dates measured, and its standing form is the catch-up invariant (a discharge VO may only still be open if it became due AFTER the last run). **A CORRECTION worth keeping, because the first reading of this was wrong:** production's 10 expired discharge VOs looked at first like the job applying the ORDINARY rule to discharge VOs — but 8 of them read **`Manual expiration`** (admins clearing them by hand, 7 on 24–25 Sep, which is the very work #3800 exists to remove) and only 2 are automatic, both `Start deadline expired` at exactly 28 days and both BEFORE #3800 shipped. Read `meta.type` and the reason text before attributing an expiry to a rule. **Traps, all confirmed on production as well as staging:** `?isDischargeManagement=` is silently IGNORED so the population must be walked with the flag read per row (the FIELD is serialized even though the filter is not), which is the file's whole runtime — production paginates `/prescriptions` at ~10 s per 100 rows, so `SINCE` is 2026-08-01 (~46 pages, ~8 min) and that is sound because an OPEN discharge VO is inside or just past a 12-day window by construction; **`order[id]` is silently ignored on `/prescription_logs`** and the collection is id-ascending, so an `order[id]=desc` "newest N" scan returns the OLDEST N — on production that is 2019-era history with no discharge reason, reading exactly like the rule never firing; the signed-treatment fields are **`rejectedTreatment` / `rejectedTreatmentWithSignature`** (the short names do not exist, so a predicate on them excludes nothing); and **`test.setTimeout()` must be called INSIDE `beforeAll`** — neither `describe.configure({ timeout })` nor the describe-level `setTimeout` reaches it, and a killed hook surfaces as `Request context disposed` on every later request, which reads like an auth failure. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.prod-discharge.page.ts`

`tests/Production/SuperAdmin/sa_ready_for_billing.spec.ts` (`@SuperAdmin @ProdReadyBilling @ReadOnly`, two tests also `@Slow`) covers **RC 3.14 #3775** (one shared ready-for-billing population behind three surfaces) and **#3785** (those rows name the VO's real status and gain the issue date and sessions done) from the PRODUCTION side. **Checked 2026-09-29: 7 passed, 0 `fixme`, 38 s.** **READ-ONLY:** every request a GET, plus navigating and reading the board. The grouped view's download is a POST and is deliberately not called, so #3785 AC5's eleven CSV columns are not checked here — its DATA is, from the payload the export is built from. **No patient data** is read or logged. Verified: **#3785 deployed** — all six Thema texts ship (three Orga, three Admin-Performance) and the combined finished/cancelled label #3785 replaced is at **0**; the grouped view paints exactly `Patient:in · VO # · Ausst. Datum · Wert · HM · Thema · Stufe · Status/Frist · Beh. Status · Empfohlene Aktion`, with `Ausst. Datum` and `Beh. Status` asserted by ORDER rather than exact equality so a later ticket adding a column does not fail it. **#3775 AC1** — the tile's **396** rows equal its reported count, span exactly the three end statuses (Fertig Behandelt 219 / Abgebrochen 131 / Abgelaufen 46) and no other, **0 of 40** sampled VOs sit in a billing batch, and **0** rows lack a `daysSince` (which is derived from the last signed treatment, so a VO without one could not carry a value — the gate's observable consequence where the count is not exposed). **AC6** with both boundary lines live: **0 rows at exactly 30 days and 1 at exactly 31**. **AC5** therapist-for-therapist against the Arbeitszeiten column — **91 therapists on both surfaces, 0 mismatches** — and scoped that way deliberately, because the two surfaces hold different therapist populations (101 vs 143) so comparing TOTALS reports a mismatch that is not one. **#3785 AC3/AC4** over the whole tile: **396/396** carry an issue date, across all three statuses, and 0 of the 328 non-Blanko rows lack a session count. **THE TRAP THAT FAILED THIS FILE'S FIRST RUN, and it is one this suite has documented twice before:** the bundle **escapes non-ASCII**, so `für` is stored as `f\xfcr` and a literal search for *"Fertig behandelt, bereit für die Abrechnung"* returns **0** — reading exactly like "never shipped" (#3337, #3611). The three Orga texts are pure ASCII and passed, the three Admin ones are not and did not; `escapedOccurrences()` now counts both forms. **A difference from staging worth knowing:** production's Blanko rows carry `totalTreatments` of **0, 1 AND 24**, where on staging every Blanko VO reported 0 — so `n / BV` keys on the Blanko FLAG, not on the total being zero, and a spec built on staging's data would encode the wrong rule. **FINDING, reported as a green test rather than a `fixme` because it is an ACCESS gate and not a defect:** `GET /kpis/admin-performance/risks` answers **403** for this Super Admin on production (against a **404** control, which is what makes it a refusal rather than a missing route), and `/me` confirms **`canAccessAdminPerformanceBoard: false`** while `canAccessTherapeutenOrgaBoard` is true. So #3775's third surface — and with it AC2 (the differing age settings), AC3 (the PKV-with-active-invoice exception) and AC5's Admin-Performance half, plus #3785 AC6's own board — cannot be verified from production with this login; they are covered on staging, where the same account can open the board. The probe re-measures each run, so the gap cannot go stale. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.prod-risk-rows-dates.page.ts`

`tests/Production/SuperAdmin/sa_monday_row_pairing.spec.ts` (`@SuperAdmin @ProdMondaySync @ReadOnly`) covers **RC 3.14 #3783** (a facility follows the Monday.com row NUMBER it keeps, not its Einrichtungs-ID) from the PRODUCTION side. **Checked 2026-09-29: 5 passed, 0 `fixme`, 8 s — and the honest headline is that DEPLOYMENT IS NOT DECIDABLE on production**, which the file proves rather than guessing at. **Read-only:** every request a GET, including the report's signed URL; the nightly sync is a console command and is never run. **Why it is undecidable, and why that is a result rather than a gap:** the only thing the fix ADDS to a run report is a `conflict` row, and rule 9 raises one only when a Monday row is kept by **two ACTIVE facilities**. Production has **4 duplicated Monday rows and every one has exactly one active facility**, so a fixed build must also report `conflict: 0` — the count carries no information. `conflictConditionExists()` asserts that absence explicitly, and the test is **falsifiable in the other direction**: the day a second active facility takes a shared row, a fixed build must emit a conflict row and the assertion flips to requiring one. On staging the same ticket dated itself precisely, because staging DOES have two active facilities sharing a row. **The four pairs are the ticket's own Out of Scope, carried out:** it says "the operations team corrects it by hand before go-live", and production's are exactly that — `E23`/`OLD-E23`, `HN1`/`OLD-HN1`, `ST 5`/`ST5`, `HH43`/`OLD-HH43`, each with the stale twin renamed and **deactivated**. So the hand-cleanup is precisely what removed the condition the fix is observable through. They are pinned as fingerprints, because "neither facility was changed" is a rule about NON-action and the only way to test it is that these rows stay put; exactly one active per shared row is asserted (two would be the conflict, zero an orphaned row). Verified positively: the sync **runs nightly on production at 19:32 UTC** (newest run 1 day old, matched 225 / created 0 / gap 40) — the control that stops every later zero being explained by the job being off; the report carries **one row per facility, 266 of 266**, which is itself #3344's regression guard, since that bug built the gap report from DEDUPLICATED Einrichtungs-IDs and produced a SHORT report in which the dropped facility appeared nowhere; **#3344's finding is CLOSED on production — 0 Einrichtungs-IDs are held by more than one facility** (on staging E23 still hides a used facility); rule 4 — **8 practices, 0 keeping a Monday row**, so none can be row-paired; and every category in the report belongs to the known vocabulary. **THE PARSING TRAP: the production CSV is COMMA-delimited.** Parsing it with `;` yields a single column per row whose NAME is the entire header line and a `category` of `undefined` for all 266 rows — reading exactly like the category column having been removed (it did here on the first pass). The parser is quote-aware because the `reason` column carries commas and quoted facility names. Also **`order[id]=desc` is REJECTED on `/sync_facility_reports`** (a non-JSON error, not a silent ignore), so the newest run is reached by paging to the end. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.prod-monday-sync.page.ts`

`tests/Production/SuperAdmin/sa_to_overview_and_grouping.spec.ts` (`@SuperAdmin @ProdToOverview @ReadOnly`, four tests also `@Slow`) covers **RC 3.14 #3770** (the working-hours table opens on the Operativ view, "Standard" becomes "Details", the heading becomes "Übersicht") and **#3774** (the risk table groups by therapist, gains its own export, and the Abrechnungs-Stau banner is retired for a plain summary line) from the PRODUCTION side. **Checked 2026-09-29: 6 passed, 0 `fixme`, 1.1 min. Both are deployed.** **Read-only:** every request a GET, plus navigating and reading the boards; #3774's export is a POST and is not called, so AC5's CSV is not checked here — its grouping is, on the screen the CSV is built from. **#3770 verified from the served dictionary and the screen, because `/status` cannot answer for a frontend-only change (#3705):** `flowBoards.arbeitszeiten` = `Übersicht`/`Overview`, `flowBoards.standard` = `Details` in BOTH locales, and `flowBoards.operativ` = **`Operativ` in both** — AC2's deliberate divergence, with `Operational` occurring **0** times. **The i18n KEYS did not change, only their values**, so a probe hunting for a `flowBoards.details` or `flowBoards.uebersicht` key finds nothing and concludes the rename never shipped. **The rename is SCOPED and that is the control**, since on a ticket whose whole diff is four dictionary lines the realistic failure is a blanket find-and-replace: `performanceDashboard.subTabs.workingHours` still reads **Arbeitszeiten** and `crm.lead_time.source_standard` still reads **Standard**. On screen the board opens on the narrow view (7 painted leaves: `Gruppe` + the bucket dot + `Effizienz / Krank % / Fertig n. abger. / Problem / Thema / Maßnahme`) against Details' **17**, with Operativ's columns a strict subset — asserted as a RANGE and a subset rather than exact counts, which is why it survived production painting `Problem / Thema` as one leaf where staging paints two. **THE READING TRAP, pinned as its own assertion:** the headers are CSS-uppercased and **German ß uppercases to SS**, so `innerText` yields `MASSNAHME` — matching neither `/Maßnahme/` nor `/Maßnahme/i`, since simple case folding does not equate ß with SS — while `textContent` keeps the shipped `Maßnahme`. #3770's own Localization Reference writes "Massnahme" with ss, i.e. the PAINTED form, so an assertion written from the AC literal fails on a correct build; both readings are asserted side by side. **#3774's API half probed by its new routes answering at all** against a **404 control**: `GET /kpis/management/unbilled-summary` **200**, `GET /kpis/orga/risks/export` **405** (registered and POST-only — a 404 would mean gone), control **404**. **AC7 lands to the cent on two different providers, which is what stops it being a tautology:** the summary serves `{count: 396, totalRevenue: 201139.52}` and the Fertig tile's 396 rows sum to **201139.52**;
